    ✓ added key in after → remove op in inverse
    ✓ removed key in after → add op in inverse with old value
    ✓ nested object change → path is JSON Pointer
  decodeBackward
    ✓ roundtrip: decodeBackward(after, encodeInverse(before, after)) === before (1 ms)
    ✓ does not mutate the input current state (1 ms)
    ✓ throws PatchConflictError when target path is missing (2 ms)
    ✓ throws PatchConflictError with opIndex on second op failure
  replayBackward
    ✓ empty chain → currentState verbatim, appliedCount 0
    ✓ applies in newest-to-oldest order (array index reversed)
    ✓ mid-chain failure → returns partial state at the failure point, never throws
    ✓ single-patch chain that fails on first op → returns currentState, appliedCount 0 (1 ms)
  deriveForward
    ✓ same state → empty patch
    ✓ replace produces forward replace with new value (1 ms)
    ✓ bidirectional consistency: applying deriveForward(before, after) to before yields after
  fast-json-patch invariants we rely on
    ✓ compare produces only add/remove/replace ops (no move/copy) (1 ms)
    ✓ decodeBackward never mutates input
    ✓ empty inverse on identical objects
  encodeInverse + decodeBackward roundtrip fuzz
    ✓ 100 random object pairs roundtrip cleanly (23 ms)
